The total normal cost rate applicable to a qualifying military service election is the rate in effect on the date the member filed the complete application for the election.</u>

<u>b. For each year or portion thereof of service credit to be credited under a qualifying military service election, the member shall pay the lesser of (i) the product of the total normal cost rate and the member's initial average compensation, increased by statutory interest from the last day of the thirty-sixth month used to determine initial average compensation, or from the last day of the final month so used if fewer than 36 months were used, to the valuation date stated in the Board's certification under subsection D of § 51.1-142.4, or (ii) the standard cost determined as of that valuation date. No statutory interest shall accrue after the valuation date, and the amount determined under this subdivision shall not change by reason of the method by which, or the date on which, payment is made or completed.</u>

<u>c. The Board shall state in the certification required by subsection D of § 51.1-142.4 the amount determined under each clause of subdivision b, the valuation date, the total normal cost rate used, and the actuarial assumptions used to determine the standard cost. A member may, once and by written request filed before the member completes payment, require the Board to redetermine both amounts as of a valuation date that is not later than the completion date stated in that certification. The Board shall make the redetermination within 30 days after receiving the request and shall notify the member in writing, and the member shall pay the lowest amount determined under either clause of subdivision b on either valuation date.</u>

<u>d. A purchase made under a qualifying military service election is not subject to the requirement that a member purchase service credit, or enter into a purchase of service credit contract, within the first 24 months of the member's active service following his first date of hire or the final day of any applicable leave of absence.</u>

<u>e. Notwithstanding subsection G, the member shall pay no less than five percent of the amount determined under subdivision b from funds other than funds paid by an employer to or on behalf of the member for the purpose of making such payment. A state agency may pay the remainder of that amount only if the state agency has first adopted a written policy governing such payments that applies to all of its employees who are eligible members as defined in § 51.1-142.4, and the state agency shall file that policy with the Board before the Board accepts any election by its employees. The policy may state a maximum aggregate amount that the state agency will pay in a fiscal year and the order in which the state agency will apply that amount, provided that the order shall be stated in the policy, shall be fixed in advance of any application of the policy, and shall be either the order in which its employees file complete applications with the Board or an order determined by lot. The state agency shall publish the policy.</u>

<u>f. The Board shall not accept payment under a qualifying military service election unless the service credit to be purchased, as credited, and the payments to be made satisfy § 415 of the Internal Revenue Code and the regulations thereunder, as determined by the Board. The Board shall make any determination under this subdivision that relates to a payment no later than 30 days after the member tenders that payment, and in every case no later than 30 days after the completion date stated in the certification. If the Board determines that it may not accept all or part of a payment for the reason stated in this subdivision, the Board shall give the member written notice of that determination, of the amount the Board may accept, and of the reasons for the determination no later than 30 days after making it, and the member may (i) reduce the service credit to be purchased to the largest amount for which the Board may accept payment, subject to subsection C of § 51.1-142.4, (ii) defer payment of the amount the Board may not accept to the earliest limitation year in which the Board may accept it, if there is a limitation year in which the Board may accept it, or (iii) cancel the election under subdivision G 5 of § 51.1-142.4. Where the member elects under clause (ii), the Board shall extend the completion date stated in the certification to a date that is no earlier than the first day of that limitation year and no later than 12 months after that day, and shall recertify the election under subdivision D 6 of § 51.1-142.4, and the reservation shall not be released under subdivision D 7 of § 51.1-142.4 by reason of the member's failure to complete payment by the completion date previously stated. No completion date, as extended one or more times under this subdivision, shall be later than 24 months after the completion date first stated in the certification, except that this sentence does not prevent the extension of a completion date to the last day of the period allowed by this subdivision for making an election under clause (i), (ii), or (iii) of this subdivision, or to a date no later than 12 months after the first day of a limitation year under clause (ii) of this subdivision. Where the Board determines that there is no limitation year in which it may accept the amount, the Board shall state that determination and the reasons for it in the notice required by this subdivision, and the member may proceed under clause (i) or clause (iii) only. In making a determination under this subdivision the Board shall apply whichever of the limitations described in § 415(n)(1) of the Internal Revenue Code permits the Board to accept the greater amount. A determination under this subdivision is not an error of the Board for the purposes of subdivision G 4 of § 51.1-142.4, and the member may seek review of it under subsection M of that section. The member may make an election under clause (i), (ii), or (iii) of this subdivision no later than 30 days after the Board gives the notice required by this subdivision, a notice being given for the purposes of this subdivision as provided in subsection A of § 51.1-142.4, and a completion date that would otherwise fall within that period is extended to the last day of that period. Nothing in this subdivision or in § 51.1-142.4 limits any right of a member under Chapter 1223 of Title 10 of the United States Code, under 38 U.S.C. § 4318, or under subsection I.</u>

<u>Except as provided in subdivision 4,</u> ~~For~~ <u>for</u> each year or portion thereof to be credited at the time of purchase under this subsection, the member shall pay the approximate normal cost of the retirement plan under which the member is covered at the time of such purchase, as determined by the Board in its sole discretion. If the member does not purchase, or enter into a purchase of service credit contract for, the service made available in this subsection within the first 24 months of the member's active service following his first date of hire or the final day of any applicable leave of absence, as applicable, then, for each year or portion thereof to be credited at the time of purchase, the member shall pay the actuarial equivalent cost. To the extent the member becomes inactive during the 24 months following his first date of hire or the final day of any applicable leave of absence, such periods shall not be included in the 24 months of active service.

Except as otherwise required by Chapter 1223 of Title 10 of the United States Code, as amended, no service credit may be purchased under this section if it is included in the calculation of any retirement allowance received or to be received by the member from this or another retirement system, or if there is a balance in a defined contribution account that serves as a primary retirement account related to such service.

For purposes of this subsection, "active duty military service" means full-time service of at least 180 consecutive days in the United States Army, Navy, Air Force, Marines, Coast Guard, or any other distinct branch of the armed services as established by the federal government, or reserve components thereof.


**C.** Any member in service may purchase all prior service credit for creditable service lost from ceasing to be a member under this chapter, as provided in § 51.1-128, because of the withdrawal of his accumulated contributions. For each year or portion thereof to be credited at the time of purchase under this subsection, the member shall pay the withdrawn amount to be purchased plus interest accrued daily and compounded annually from the date of withdrawal to the date of payment at the assumed rate of return established by the Board for the actuarial valuation of the retirement system that is in effect at the time of the purchase. The Board shall develop guidelines and procedures for administering this subsection.

**D.** Any member in service may purchase service credit for accumulated sick leave on his effective date of retirement based upon such sums as the employer may provide as payment for any unused sick leave balances. The cost of service credit purchased under this subsection shall be the actuarial equivalent cost of such service.

**E.** Any member receiving benefits under the Virginia Workers' Compensation Act (§ 65.2-100 et seq.) may, in a manner prescribed by the Board and prior to the effective date of retirement, purchase service that is not reported to the retirement system by the member's employer while the member is receiving such benefits.

For each year or portion thereof to be credited at the time of purchase under this subsection, the member shall pay the approximate normal cost of the retirement plan under which the member is covered, as determined by the Board in its sole discretion. If the member does not purchase, or enter into a purchase of service credit contract for, any service made available in this subsection within the first 24 months of the member's active service following his first date of hire or the final day of any applicable leave of absence, then, for each year or portion thereof to be credited at the time of purchase, the member shall pay the actuarial equivalent cost. To the extent the member becomes inactive during the 24 months following his first date of hire or the final day of any applicable leave of absence, such periods shall not be included in the 24 months of active service.

**F.** Payment may be made in a lump sum at the time of purchase or by payroll deduction. Any number of additional deductions may be permitted at any time. Should any deduction be terminated before the member purchases the entire period contracted for, the member shall be credited with the number of full or partial months of service for which full payment has been made. If any deduction is continued after the entire period has been purchased, the member shall be credited with no more than the amount of service for which he was eligible and for which he paid, and the excess amount deducted shall be refunded to the member.

**G.** Any employer may elect to pay an equivalent amount in lieu of all member contributions required of its employees for the purchase of service credit pursuant to this section. These contributions shall not be considered wages for purposes of Chapter 7 (§ 51.1-700 et seq.), nor shall they be considered salary for purposes of this chapter.

**H.** In any case where member and employer contributions, as required under this chapter, were not made because of an error in the payroll, personnel, or other classification system of an employer participating in the retirement system, service that has not been credited because of such error may be purchased on the following basis:

1. The most recent three years of service credit shall be purchased, using applicable member and employer contribution rates and creditable compensation in effect for such period, in a manner and at the cost prescribed by the Board; and

2. All other years of service credit shall be purchased by the employer at an actuarial equivalent cost.

**I.** Any member may receive credit at no cost for service rendered in the armed forces of the United States provided (i) the member was on leave of absence from a covered position, (ii) the discharge from a period of active duty with the armed forces was not dishonorable, (iii) the member has not withdrawn his accumulated contributions, (iv) the member is not disabled or killed while on leave without pay while performing active duty military service in the armed forces of the United States, and (v) the member reenters service in a covered position within one year after discharge from the armed forces. In order to receive such service, the member must complete such forms and other requirements as are required by the Board and the retirement system.

---

### <u>§ 51.1-142.4. Military service purchase pilot program for state employees.</u>

<u>**A.** As used in this section:</u>

<u>"Certified subsidy" means the amount certified by the Board under subsection D for an election, which shall be the amount by which (i) the actuarial present value, determined as of the valuation date stated in the certification, of the benefits payable under this chapter to the member and to the member's survivors and beneficiaries, taking account of the service credit to be purchased and of the member's retirement on the member's anticipated earliest qualifying retirement date, exceeds (ii) the expected value, taken over the distribution of the member's date of retirement projected as required by this definition, of the actuarial present value, determined as of the valuation date stated in the certification, of the benefits payable under this chapter to the member and to the member's survivors and beneficiaries without the service credit to be purchased, less (iii) the amounts payable by the member and by any state agency under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2. The Board shall determine each actuarial present value using the actuarial assumptions adopted by the Board for the most recent actuarial valuation of the retirement system. For the purposes of clause (ii), the Board shall project the member's date of retirement by applying, as annual probabilities over the member's remaining working lifetime, the rates of retirement in those actuarial assumptions, together with the rates of mortality in those assumptions and no other decrement. No rate shall be applied under clause (ii) as though the member's retirement on any date were certain, and the date of retirement projected under clause (ii) shall not be determined by reference to any date on which the member first becomes eligible for a service retirement allowance, whether reduced or unreduced and whether determined with or without the service credit to be purchased. The certified subsidy shall not be less than the amount by which the standard cost of the service credit to be purchased, determined as of the valuation date stated in the certification, exceeds the amounts payable by the member and by any state agency under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2. For the purposes of clause (i), the member's anticipated earliest qualifying retirement date is the first day of the first month following the month that includes the completion date stated in the certification. The Board shall redetermine the certified subsidy as of the member's effective date of retirement as required by subdivision G 1, and the amount so redetermined shall not exceed the amount last certified and reserved for the election.</u>

<u>"Complete application" means an application for an election under this section that contains every item of information and every certification that the Board requires by procedures adopted and published under subsection M. The Board shall not require under those procedures any item that is not necessary to determine the member's eligibility, the service credit the member may purchase under subsection C, the amount payable by the member under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2, or the certified subsidy.</u>

<u>"Earliest qualifying retirement date" means the first day of the month following the month in which the Board credits to the member all of the service credit purchased under the member's election. Where the Board credits that service credit as of a date stated in a request under subsection E, the month in which the Board credits it is the month that includes the date so stated.</u>

<u>"Election" means a qualifying military service election as defined in subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2.</u>

<u>"Eligible member" means a member in service who (i) is a state employee, (ii) is covered under the retirement plan established by this chapter, whether or not the member is a person described in clause (i) or (ii) of subdivision A 1 of § 51.1-155, (iii) does not participate in the hybrid retirement program established by § 51.1-169, (iv) is eligible to purchase service credit for prior active duty military service under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2, (v) has not reached his unreduced retirement eligibility date, determined without the service credit to be purchased, on the date the member files a complete application, taking account of all creditable service for which the member is entitled to credit as of that date, (vi) would, if the member's maximum purchasable amount determined as of the date the member files that application were credited to the member on that date, reach his unreduced retirement eligibility date on or before the first day of the month following the month in which the member files that application, (vii) would, if the least of the member's maximum purchasable amount and the amount specified in clause (ii) of subsection C, each determined as of the date the member files that application, or, where no amount is specified in clause (ii) of subsection C, the member's maximum purchasable amount determined as of that date, were credited to the member on that date, reach his unreduced retirement eligibility date at least one month earlier than the date on which the member would reach his unreduced retirement eligibility date determined without that service credit, and (viii) would reach his unreduced retirement eligibility date, determined with the service credit to be purchased, on or before the first day of the month following the month in which that service credit is credited to the member. For the purposes of clause (vii) of this definition, a date is at least one month earlier than a second date if it is on or before the day, of the calendar month preceding the month in which that second date falls, that corresponds in number to the day of that second date, or, where that preceding month has no corresponding day, on or before the first day of the month in which that second date falls. "Eligible member" does not include a member of a retirement system established under Chapter 2 (§ 51.1-200 et seq.), Chapter 2.1 (§ 51.1-211 et seq.), or Chapter 3 (§ 51.1-300 et seq.), a teacher, an employee of a political subdivision, the Governor, the Lieutenant Governor, the Attorney General, a member of the General Assembly, or any person whose retirement allowance would be payable under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-155.</u>

<u>"Final qualifying retirement date" means the date that is 12 months after the member's earliest qualifying retirement date, except that the Board shall approve a later date where the Board's own processing requirements, or any other cause not attributable to the member, make the member's retirement on or before the date that would otherwise apply under this definition impracticable. A later date approved under this definition shall be the first day of a month and shall be not more than three months after the date determined under the first sentence of this definition. The Board shall approve a later date on its own initiative or upon the written request of the member, which the member may file at any time before the date that would otherwise apply under this definition or before any later date approved under this definition; the Board shall act upon such a request, and shall give the member written notice of its determination and of the reasons for it, no later than 30 days after the request is filed; and the member may seek review of the determination under subsection M. The Board shall determine the member's earliest qualifying retirement date and final qualifying retirement date, and shall state both of them and the reasons for any later date approved under this definition, in writing to the member no later than 15 days after the Board credits to the member the service credit purchased under the member's election, and shall give the member written notice of any later date approved after that statement no later than 15 days after the approval. Where the member's final qualifying retirement date as extended under subdivision D 1 or as tolled under subsection M is not the first day of a month, the member's final qualifying retirement date is the first day of the next month. In no event shall a member's final qualifying retirement date, as extended under subdivision D 1 or as tolled under subsection M, be later than 12 months after the date that applies under the first sentence of this definition or, where a later date has been approved under this definition, after that later date, except that this sentence does not apply to an extension under subdivision D 1 by reason of a statement or notice given later than the time required by this definition, and the aggregate of all extensions so excluded shall not exceed 12 months. Where the running of a period has been tolled under subsection M and the member is prevented by the preceding sentence from retiring in a qualifying retirement after that tolling ends, the member's final qualifying retirement date is, notwithstanding the preceding sentence, the first day of the second month following the month in which the Board last acts upon a request the filing of which tolled that period, and in no event later than 12 months after the latest date the preceding sentence permits. Notwithstanding the two preceding sentences, where the date that would otherwise apply under this definition is earlier than the first day of the second month following the month in which the Board gives the statement required by this definition, the member's final qualifying retirement date is that first day. Where the Board does not act upon a request the filing of which tolled the running of the member's final qualifying retirement date under subsection M within the period this definition allows for acting upon it, and the date that would otherwise apply under this definition is earlier than the first day of the second month following the month in which that period expired, the member's final qualifying retirement date is, notwithstanding any other provision of this definition, that first day. Where the Board's action upon a request determines a member's final qualifying retirement date under this definition, the Board shall state that date in its decision upon the request, and where the Board does not do so it shall give the member written notice of that date no later than 15 days after it acts upon the request. Where the date that would otherwise apply under this definition is earlier than the first day of the second month following the month in which the Board gives a decision or notice required by the preceding sentence, the member's final qualifying retirement date is, notwithstanding any other provision of this definition, that first day.</u>

<u>"Involuntary separation" means an involuntary separation as defined in subsection B of § 2.2-3200. "Involuntary separation" does not include a separation for reasons related to the job performance or misconduct of the member or a voluntary resignation. The date of an involuntary separation is the date on which the member's employment terminates or, where the member is placed on leave without pay-layoff or equivalent status, the date of that placement, or, in the case of any other involuntary separation, the date on which the member ceases to occupy the position from which the member is separated.</u>

<u>"Maximum purchasable amount" means, with respect to a member and a date, the least of (a) the amount of service credit the member is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 on that date, (b) the amount of service credit specified in clause (i) of subsection C on that date, and (c) the amount of service credit specified in clause (iii) of subsection C on that date. Clause (ii) of subsection C does not limit the maximum purchasable amount.</u>

<u>"Program" means the pilot program established by this section.</u>

<u>"Qualifying retirement" means a service retirement under § 51.1-153, or under subsection A or C of § 51.1-155.1, with an effective date of retirement that is the first day of a month, is no earlier than the member's earliest qualifying retirement date, and is no later than the member's final qualifying retirement date, the allowance for which retirement is calculated in accordance with subdivision A 1 of § 51.1-155 and is not reduced under subdivision A 2 or A 3 of that section, in each case determined as though no option had been elected under § 51.1-165 or § 51.1-165.01 and no lump-sum distribution had been paid under § 51.1-165.01. Where the Board's processing requirements delay the commencement of payment of the member's retirement allowance beyond the effective date of retirement, the allowance shall be payable retroactively to that effective date.</u>

<u>"Standard cost" means standard cost as defined in subdivision B 4 a of § 51.1-142.2. A reference in this section to the standard cost of an amount of service credit is a reference to the aggregate of the standard cost for each year or portion thereof of that service credit.</u>

<u>"State agency" means any agency, authority, board, bureau, commission, department, or institution of the Commonwealth, including a public institution of higher education and an agency of the legislative or judicial branch.</u>

<u>"Unreduced retirement eligibility date" means the earliest date on which the member may retire under § 51.1-153, or under subsection A or C of § 51.1-155.1, and receive a service retirement allowance that is calculated in accordance with subdivision A 1 of § 51.1-155 and is not reduced under subdivision A 2 or A 3 of that section, in each case determined as though no option had been elected under § 51.1-165 or § 51.1-165.01 and no lump-sum distribution had been paid under § 51.1-165.01. For the purposes of this section the member may retire under subsection A or C of § 51.1-155.1 only where the member has incurred an involuntary separation, or the member's office has been lawfully abolished, as the case may be, on or before the date as of which the member's unreduced retirement eligibility date is determined or, where this sentence is applied otherwise than in the determination of the member's unreduced retirement eligibility date, on or before the member's effective date of retirement. Where this section does not state a date as of which the member's unreduced retirement eligibility date is determined, that date is, for the purposes of the preceding sentence, the date on which the Board makes the certification, recertification, determination, statement, or other decision in which that date is applied.</u>

<u>For the purposes of this section and of subdivision B 4 f of § 51.1-142.2, a written statement, notice, or certification required of the Board is given on the date the Board mails it to the person to whom it is required to be given or transmits it electronically to an address that person has provided to the Board for that purpose, except that an electronic transmission completed after 5:00 p.m. is given on the following day. For the purposes of this section and of subdivision B 4 f of § 51.1-142.2, where the Board mails a written statement, notice, or certification, every period within which a person may or must act that runs from the giving of that statement, notice, or certification is extended by three days.</u>

<u>**B.** The Program is established. An eligible member may purchase service credit for prior active duty military service under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 at the cost determined under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2 if, and only if, the Board has accepted the member's election under subsection D. No person whom the definition of "eligible member" in subsection A excludes, including a teacher, an employee of a political subdivision, and a member of a retirement system established under Chapter 2 (§ 51.1-200 et seq.), Chapter 2.1 (§ 51.1-211 et seq.), or Chapter 3 (§ 51.1-300 et seq.), is eligible to make an election under this section. For the avoidance of doubt, service credit purchased under an election is prior service purchased under § 51.1-142.2, is creditable service as defined in § 51.1-124.3, and shall be treated for all purposes of this chapter in the same manner as other service credit purchased under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2. Participation in the Program is voluntary, and no employer shall require, request, or induce any employee to make an election.</u>

<u>**C.** An eligible member may purchase under an election no more service credit than the least of (i) four years, less any service credit previously purchased by the member under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2; (ii) the number of months of creditable service the member needs in order to have reached his unreduced retirement eligibility date as of the filing date of the member's complete application, taking account of all creditable service for which the member is entitled to credit as of that date, any fraction of a month in the number so determined being counted as a whole month; and (iii) the number of years of creditable service the member has earned as a state employee. The Board shall determine each of these amounts no later than 30 days after the member's application is completed and shall state them, the filing date of the member's complete application, and the completion date, in the certification required by subsection D. The Board shall not accept an election for which the amount determined under clause (ii) of this subsection is zero. Where the amount determined under clause (ii) of this subsection is zero, the Board shall give the member written notice of that determination, of the reasons for it, of the member's rights under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2, and of the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the member is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date, no later than 30 days after the member's application is completed, and the member may seek review of the determination under subsection M. Where no number of months of creditable service would cause the member to have reached his unreduced retirement eligibility date as of the filing date of the member's complete application, clause (ii) of this subsection does not limit the service credit the member may purchase, and the Board shall, in place of determining and stating an amount under that clause, determine, no later than 30 days after the member's application is completed, that no amount is specified in that clause and shall state that determination in the certification required by subsection D. A determination that no amount is specified in clause (ii) of this subsection is not a determination that the amount under that clause is zero. Where no amount is specified in clause (ii) of this subsection, the amount determined under that clause is not zero for the purposes of this subsection. The Board shall state in the report required by subsection J every failure to make a determination, to state a matter this subsection requires it to state, or to give a notice, required by this subsection within the period this subsection allows for it. Where the Board does not give the notice required by this subsection within that period, the member may seek review under subsection M of the Board's failure to give it at any time before the Board gives it, and the period within which the member may seek review of a determination under this subsection runs from the date the Board gives that notice.</u>

<u>**D. 1.** The Board shall not accept an election until the Board has, in writing, certified to the member and reserved the certified subsidy for that election under this subsection. The Board shall determine the certified subsidy for an election no later than 30 days after the member's application is completed. The Board shall give the certification required by this subsection no later than 15 days after the Director of the Department of Planning and Budget or his designee acts upon the Board's request under subdivision 3. The Board's certification shall state the matters required by subdivision B 4 c of § 51.1-142.2, the filing date of the member's complete application, the amount of service credit to be purchased, the certified subsidy, the valuation date, the completion date by which the member must complete payment, the member's anticipated earliest qualifying retirement date and anticipated final qualifying retirement date, the member's unreduced retirement eligibility date determined with the service credit to be purchased as certified, the member's right to cancel the election under subdivision G 5 at any time before completing payment, and the member's obligations under subsections E, F, and G. The Board shall not accept an election until the member has filed with the Board a written acknowledgment of the member's obligations under subsections E, F, and G. The Board's certification shall also state that, where a member whose election the Board has accepted dies without having retired, the benefit payable on the member's death is determined under subdivision G 4 and under § 51.1-162 as on the death of a member in service and not under the provisions of subdivision C 1 of § 51.1-165 or of subsection F of § 51.1-165.01 that require benefits to be paid in accordance with § 51.1-163; that where the member dies while in service and subdivision C 2 of § 51.1-165 otherwise applies, that subdivision provides for the payment of benefits in accordance with § 51.1-163, except that where the contingent annuitant or annuitants under the option elected are the same person or persons who would receive a monthly benefit under subsection B of § 51.1-162 and that monthly benefit would be greater, that subdivision provides for the payment of benefits in accordance with that subsection, and that the Board shall pay the benefit that subdivision provides or the benefit determined under subdivision G 4, whichever is greater; that no option elected under subdivision A 2 or A 3 of § 51.1-165 limits that benefit; that subsection C of § 51.1-165.01 provides that any optional payment of the retirement allowance pursuant to § 51.1-165 is based upon the retirement allowance as reduced to reflect a lump-sum distribution elected under that section; but that the straight life option under subdivision A 1 of § 51.1-165 is an election to receive an increased retirement allowance in lieu of any death benefits and, where that election is not rendered null and void by subdivision C 1 of § 51.1-165, may leave no benefit payable on the member's death; that, to the extent required by § 401(a)(37) of the Internal Revenue Code, as amended or renumbered, and the regulations thereunder applicable to governmental plans, subsection B of § 51.1-162 provides a retirement allowance on the death of a member in service only where no benefit is payable under subsection C of that section and only to a surviving spouse, minor child, or parent whom the member has designated under subsection A of that section or who is entitled in the order of precedence that subsection B of that section states where the member has made no designation or the person designated has predeceased the member and no other designation has been made; that subsection C of § 51.1-162 provides a retirement allowance, where the member dies in service from a cause compensable under the Virginia Workers' Compensation Act (§ 65.2-100 et seq.), to the member's surviving spouse, minor children, or parents as that subsection provides; that where no benefit is payable under subsection B of § 51.1-162, subsection A of that section provides for the payment of the member's accumulated contributions, reduced by the amount of any retirement allowance previously received by the member under this chapter or the abolished system, to the person the member has designated or, where the member has made no designation or the person designated has predeceased the member and no other designation has been made, to a surviving relative in the order of precedence that section states; and that the Board advises the member to review the member's designation of beneficiary under subsection A of § 51.1-162, and the effect of that designation under subsection B of that section, before completing payment, that the member may make or change that designation under subsection A of that section at any time before the member's death, and that nothing in this subdivision requires the member to review the designation or affects any benefit by reason of the member's not having reviewed it. A failure to state in the Board's certification every matter this subdivision requires to be stated in it, other than a failure to state that the Board advises the member to review the member's designation of beneficiary, is a failure of the Board to state in a certification a matter this section requires to be stated in it for the purposes of subsection M; a person adversely affected by it may seek review under subsection M no later than 30 days after the date on which the certification is given to the member or, where that person is not the member, no later than 30 days after the date on which the notice required by subdivision G 4 is given to that person; the second sentence of subdivision M 2 does not apply to such a failure where the person adversely affected is the member; and the Board shall state every such failure, and every failure to state that the Board advises the member to review the member's designation of beneficiary, in the report required by subsection J. The member shall file the notice of retirement required by § 51.1-153, stating as the effective date of retirement a date on which the member will retire in a qualifying retirement, no later than 30 days after the date on which the Board gives the member the written statement of the member's earliest qualifying retirement date and final qualifying retirement date first required by the definition of "final qualifying retirement date" in subsection A. Nothing in this subdivision requires the member to file that notice any period before the effective date of retirement stated in it. A notice filed within the period allowed by this subdivision satisfies § 51.1-153 notwithstanding that it is filed after the effective date of retirement stated in it; and where such a notice is filed after that effective date, the member's retirement shall take effect on that date and the retirement allowance shall be payable retroactively to that date, subject to subsection E of § 51.1-153 and provided that the effective date stated is on or after the day following the member's last day of service. The Board shall state the period allowed by this subdivision in the written statement required by that definition. Where the Board gives that written statement, any notice of a later approved date required by that definition, or any notice of a date required by that definition upon the Board's action upon a request, later than the time required by that definition, the member's final qualifying retirement date is extended by the number of days by which the statement or notice is late, the member may state an effective date of retirement that is no earlier than the first day of the month following the month in which the Board gives the statement or notice, and the Board shall state every such delay in the report required by subsection J. The Board shall state, in the written statement required by that definition and in every notice of a later approved date required by that definition, (i) that where the Board gives the statement or notice later than the time required by that definition the member's final qualifying retirement date is extended by the number of days by which the statement or notice is late, that the date so extended is subject to the limits stated in that definition, and that where the date so extended is not the first day of a month it is the first day of the next month; (ii) that the member may state as the effective date of retirement a date no earlier than the first day of the month following the month in which the Board gives the statement or notice, and that nothing in this subdivision prevents the member from stating an earlier effective date of retirement to which the member is otherwise entitled, subject to subsection E of § 51.1-153; and (iii) that where the Board mails the statement or notice, every period that runs from the giving of it is extended by three days.</u>

<u>2. The Board shall not accept an election if the certified subsidy for that election, together with the contingency amount required by subdivision 3 for that election and together with all certified subsidies and all contingency amounts previously reserved under this subsection and not released, would exceed $3,000,000. A certified subsidy that has been settled shall not be released, except to the extent required by subdivision G 1 or G 4, and the amount settled shall remain charged against the limitation in the first sentence of this subdivision. The Board shall not accept an election for which the certified subsidy exceeds $170,000. Where the certified subsidy determined for an election exceeds $170,000, the Board shall give the applicant written notice of the Board's determination that it may not accept the election, of the reasons for that determination, of the certified subsidy determined for the election, of the applicant's rights under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2, and of the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the applicant is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date, no later than 30 days after the applicant's application is completed, and the applicant may seek review of the determination under subsection M. The limitations in this subdivision are self-executing and require no further act of the General Assembly, the Board, or any other person. A certification that the certified subsidy for an election is zero or less than zero shall not increase the amount available to be reserved for any other election.</u>

<u>3. Before reserving a certified subsidy the Board shall obtain the written confirmation of the Director of the Department of Planning and Budget, or his designee, that funds appropriated and legally available for the purposes of this section are sufficient to fund the reservation. The Board shall file the request required by this subdivision no later than 15 days after the member's application is completed. The Director or his designee shall act upon the Board's request within 30 days after the request is filed. If the Director or his designee does not act within that period, the Board shall give written notice of that fact, and of the date of the Board's request, to the member and to the Chairmen of the House Committee on Appropriations and the Senate Committee on Finance and Appropriations no later than 15 days after that period expires, and shall state every such lapse in the report required by subsection J. An application for which a request under this subdivision is pending retains its filing date for the purposes of subdivisions 4 and 5, and the Board shall act upon the application no later than 15 days after the confirmation is obtained. Where a request under this subdivision has been pending for 90 days, the Board shall give the applicant written notice of that fact, of the applicant's rights under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2, and of the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the applicant is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date. If the Director or his designee determines that funds appropriated and legally available are not sufficient to fund the reservation, the Board shall place the application on a waiting list in the order determined under subdivision 4, shall give the applicant the notice required by subdivision 5, shall give written notice of the determination and of the reasons stated for it to the Chairmen of the House Committee on Appropriations and the Senate Committee on Finance and Appropriations no later than 15 days after the determination, shall state every such determination in the report required by subsection J, and shall request a further confirmation under this subdivision no less often than annually and no later than 30 days after the general appropriation act making appropriations for that fiscal year becomes law, until the application is accepted, the applicant withdraws it, or this section expires. A placement on the waiting list under this subdivision is a determination of the Board for the purposes of subsection M. The Board shall also reserve, at the time it reserves each certified subsidy, a contingency amount equal to `[20]` percent of that certified subsidy, to fund any recertification required by subdivision 6. A contingency amount reserved under this subdivision is charged against the limitation in subdivision 2, and the Board shall release it when the certified subsidy is settled under subdivision G 1 or G 4 or the reservation is released under subdivision 7. A reservation shall remain in effect until the certified subsidy is settled under subdivision G 1 or G 4 or the reservation is released under subdivision 7. The Board shall obtain a further written confirmation under this subdivision for each fiscal year in which any reservation remains in effect; until that confirmation is obtained the Board shall accept no further election, but the absence of the confirmation shall not release any reservation, shall not affect any election the Board has accepted, and shall not affect any service credit the Board has credited.</u>

<u>4. The Board shall accept elections in the order in which eligible members file complete applications. The Board shall give written notice to each applicant, within 30 days after the application is filed, stating whether the application is complete and, if it is not, identifying every item that is missing; the applicant shall have 60 days from that notice to supply the missing items, and an application completed within that period retains the filing date of the original application for all purposes of this section, including the determination of eligibility required by this subdivision. Where the Board does not give the notice required by this subdivision within 30 days after the application is filed, the applicant shall have 60 days from the date the Board gives the notice to supply the missing items, an application completed within that period or before the Board gives the notice retains the filing date of the original application for all purposes of this section, and the Board shall state every such delay in the report required by subsection J. Where two or more complete applications bear the same filing date and the amount available to be reserved is insufficient for all of them, the Board shall determine the order among them by lot. The Board shall determine whether an applicant is an eligible member as of the filing date of the applicant's complete application and shall give the applicant written notice of the determination no later than 30 days after the application is completed. Where the Board determines that an applicant is not an eligible member, the notice shall state the reasons for the determination, shall state the applicant's rights under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2 and the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the applicant is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date, and shall state that the applicant may file a further application; and the applicant may seek review of the determination under subsection M. The Board shall accept applications through at least two channels, one of which shall not require the use of an electronic system, and shall publish on its website the amount remaining available to be reserved, updated no less often than monthly. An applicant may withdraw an application at any time before the Board accepts the election by giving the Board written notice of the withdrawal, and the withdrawal is effective on the date the Board receives the notice.</u>

<u>5. When the amount available to be reserved is insufficient for the certified subsidy of a complete application, the Board shall place the application on a waiting list in the order determined under subdivision 4, shall give the applicant written notice of that fact and of the reasons for it, and shall accept the election if and when sufficient amounts become available before the expiration of this section. The Board shall not accept an election, and no member shall acquire any right to purchase service credit at the cost determined under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2, before the Board has reserved the certified subsidy. An applicant who is denied or wait-listed retains every right to purchase service credit under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2 at the standard cost, and the Board shall state that right, and the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the applicant is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date, in the notice required by subdivision 4 or by this subdivision, as the case may be. No later than 30 days before this section expires, the Board shall give written notice to each applicant then remaining on the waiting list stating that the application will not be accepted, stating the applicant's rights under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2, and stating the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the applicant is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date.</u>

<u>6. If, before the member completes payment, the member's creditable compensation, creditable service, or anticipated effective date of retirement changes, or is corrected, such that the certified subsidy would increase or such that the service credit the member may purchase under subsection C would change, or if the member requests a redetermination under subdivision B 4 c of § 51.1-142.2, the Board shall recertify the certified subsidy and the service credit to be purchased and shall reserve any increase in the certified subsidy, in each case before the member completes payment. If the amount available to be reserved, including the contingency amount reserved under subdivision 3, is insufficient to fund the increase, the Board shall so notify the member in writing within 30 days and the member may (i) reduce the amount of service credit to be purchased, subject to subsection C, (ii) pay the amount of the increase, or (iii) cancel the election under subdivision G 5. The Board shall give the member written notice of every recertification under this subdivision, of the amounts as recertified, and of the reasons for the recertification, no later than 30 days after making it, and the member may seek review of the recertification under subsection M. Where a recertification under this subdivision reduces the amount of service credit the member may purchase under clause (ii) of subsection C to zero, the member's election is cancelled, the Board shall refund every amount paid under the election, shall release the reservation and the contingency amount reserved for it, and shall give the member written notice of the cancellation, of the reasons for it, of the member's rights under subsection B of § 51.1-142.2, and of the standard cost, determined as of the date of the notice, of the service credit, if any, the member is eligible to purchase under subdivision B 2 of § 51.1-142.2 as of that date, no later than 30 days after making the recertification, and the member may seek review of the cancellation under subsection M. Where a recertification under this subdivision reduces the amount of service credit the member may purchase but not to zero, the service credit to be purchased is reduced to the amount the member may purchase as recertified, and the Board shall refund any amount paid that exceeds the amount payable as so recertified. Where the member reduces the amount of service credit to be purchased under clause (i) of this subdivision or under clause (i) of subdivision B 4 f of § 51.1-142.2, the service credit to be purchased is reduced to the amount to which the member reduces it, the Board shall recertify the certified subsidy and the amount payable to reflect the reduction, and the Board shall refund any amount paid that exceeds the amount payable as so recertified. The Board shall pay a refund required by this subdivision to the member and to any state agency from whose appropriation an amount was paid on the member's behalf, in proportion to the amounts each paid, within 60 days after the recertification, except that to the extent a refund may not be paid without adverse federal tax consequence the Board shall apply that amount to the purchase of additional service credit for the member at the standard cost and shall notify the member in writing of the amount so applied. An error by the Board in a certification shall not create any obligation of the member to pay any amount beyond the amount certified, and the Board shall correct the error and reserve any resulting increase from amounts available under subdivision 2.</u>

<u>7. A reservation expires, and the Board shall release the reserved amount, if the member has not completed payment by the completion date stated in the certification, if the member cancels the election, if the member fails to retire as required by subsection F, if the member's election is cancelled under subdivision 6, or, to the extent of any reduction, if the member reduces the service credit to be purchased under subdivision 6 or if a recertification under subdivision 6 reduces the certified subsidy. A reservation shall not be released under this subdivision by reason of the member's failure to retire as required by subsection F, or by reason of the member's failure to complete payment by the completion date, where the service credit purchased under the election vests under subdivision G 4; and a reservation shall not be released under this subdivision by reason of the member's failure to complete payment by the completion date previously stated where the member has made an election under clause (ii) of subdivision B 4 f of § 51.1-142.2 or where the period allowed by that subdivision for making such an election has not expired. The Board shall give the member written notice of the expiration and of the reasons for it. The Board shall release a reserved certified subsidy and its contingency amount upon giving the notice required by subsection C or by subdivision D 2.</u>

<u>**E.** The member shall complete payment of the amount determined under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2 by the completion date stated in the Board's certification, which shall be no later than 12 months after the date the Board accepts the election. Payment may be made by any method authorized by subsection F of § 51.1-142.2 and by the Board's procedures, provided that the aggregate of all payments shall equal the amount stated in the certification and no additional amount shall be charged by reason of the method or the timing of payment. The Board shall credit the purchased service credit to the member only upon receipt of payment in full, and shall do so within 30 days after receipt of payment in full. The member may request in writing, before or together with the final payment, that the Board credit the service credit as of the last day of a month stated in the request that is not later than 30 days after the date on which the member expects to make the final payment; the Board shall credit the service credit as of the date so stated if payment in full is received on or before that date, and in every other case shall credit it within 30 days after receipt of payment in full. In every case the Board shall give the member written notice of the date as of which it credits the service credit no later than five days after it credits it. No service credit purchased under an election shall be credited before payment in full is received, except as subdivision G 4 provides in the case of the member's death, of the member's approval for a disability retirement allowance, or of an error of the Board, and, except for the purposes of the definition of "eligible member" in subsection A, no member shall be treated as having reached his unreduced retirement eligibility date by reason of an election before payment in full is received.</u>

<u>**F.** A member whose election the Board has accepted shall retire in a qualifying retirement on or before the member's final qualifying retirement date. A member who does not retire in a qualifying retirement shall be subject to subsection G, and no other consequence shall attach. No member shall be liable to the retirement system, to an employer, or to the Commonwealth for any amount by reason of a failure to retire in a qualifying retirement, and no amount shall be collected, offset, or withheld from any member, retiree, or beneficiary on that account. Nothing required or permitted by subdivision G 2, G 3, G 4, or G 5, including any deferral of payment of a retirement allowance that subdivision G 2 requires until a determination under that subdivision takes effect, the reduction of credited service required by clause (i) of subdivision G 2, and the deferral of a refund, and nothing required by subdivision D 6, G 2, G 4, or G 5 by way of the application of a refund to the purchase of service credit where the refund may not be paid without adverse federal tax consequence, is a collection, offset, or withholding for the purposes of this section, and no amount payable to the member or to any survivor, beneficiary, or estate of the member shall be reduced by reason of any such deferral or application.</u>

<u>**G. 1.** The certified subsidy for an election is settled, and the service credit purchased under the election vests at the cost determined under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2, on the member's effective date of retirement in a qualifying retirement. The Board shall, within 90 days after that date, redetermine the certified subsidy in the manner provided in the definition of that term in subsection A but substituting the member's actual effective date of retirement for the member's anticipated earliest qualifying retirement date, and the amount settled shall be the amount so redetermined or the amount last certified and reserved for the election, whichever is less. The Board shall release the amount by which the amount reserved for the election, together with the contingency amount reserved under subdivision D 3, exceeds the amount settled. A redetermination under this subdivision shall not create any obligation of the member, of any beneficiary, or of any employer, shall not reduce the service credit credited to the member, and shall not affect the member's retirement allowance. No obligation of the member arises after the member's effective date of retirement by reason of the election.</u>

<u>2. If the member does not retire in a qualifying retirement, the Board shall, except as subdivision 4 provides, within 60 days after the earlier of (a) the member's final qualifying retirement date, if any, and (b) the date on which the member retires in a retirement that is not a qualifying retirement, (i) reduce the service credit credited to the member under the election to the largest number of whole months of service credit that the member's payments would have purchased at the standard cost determined as of the date the Board accepted the election, (ii) refund to the member, and to any state agency from whose appropriation an amount was paid on the member's behalf, in proportion to the amounts each paid, the amount by which the amounts paid exceed the cost, at that standard cost, of the service credit credited under clause (i), and (iii) release the reservation. The Board shall give the member written notice of the determination, of the service credit retained, and of the reasons for the determination, no less than 30 days, and in no case less than the period allowed to the member by subsection M within which to request reconsideration of the determination, before the determination takes effect, and a determination that takes effect in contravention of this sentence takes effect instead on the first day on which it may take effect under this sentence; and the member may seek review of the determination under subsection M. The Board shall not pay any retirement allowance to a member whose election has not been settled under subdivision 1 or 4 until the Board has made the determination required by this subdivision, and the determination shall take effect before the first payment of any retirement allowance to the member; and the first payment of the retirement allowance shall include every amount that would have been payable to the member from the effective date of retirement had the determination been made before the effective date of retirement. Nothing in this subdivision prevents the payment of a retirement allowance to a member while an application of the member for a disability retirement allowance under this chapter is pending, and the determination required by this subdivision shall take effect notwithstanding that a retirement allowance has been paid to the member while such an application was pending. No determination under this subdivision is required in respect of an election that has been cancelled under subdivision D 6 or under subdivision 5 of this subsection, in respect of an election under which the member has been credited with service credit under subdivision 3 or 5 of this subsection, or in respect of an election under which the reservation has been released under subdivision D 7 without any service credit having been credited to the member, and no retirement allowance shall be withheld by reason of this subdivision on account of any such election. For the purposes of this subdivision, "the determination date" means the date from which the 60-day period within which the Board must make the determination required by this subdivision runs, whether that period runs under the first sentence of this subdivision or under subdivision 4. The Board shall give the notice required by this subdivision no later than 60 days after the determination date, and shall state in the report required by subsection J every failure to give that notice within that period. A determination under this subdivision shall take effect no later than 60 days after the Board gives the notice required by this subdivision, and in no event later than 120 days after the determination date, except that where the Board gives that notice later than 60 days after the determination date, or has not given it within 60 days after the determination date, the determination takes effect on the first day on which it may take effect under the sentence of this subdivision that requires notice to be given no less than 30 days before the determination takes effect, and in that case the limit of 120 days stated in this sentence does not apply. Where the member dies before the first payment of the retirement allowance, the Board shall pay the amount that would have been included in that payment under this subdivision to the person entitled to any benefit payable on the member's death or, if there is none, to the member's estate. The Board shall pay a refund required by this subdivision within 60 days after the determination takes effect, except that to the extent a refund may not be paid to the member without adverse federal tax consequence to the member, the Board shall apply that amount to the purchase of additional service credit for the member at the standard cost and shall notify the member in writing of the amount so applied.</u>

<u>3. If the member completes payment for part only of the service credit stated in the Board's certification, the Board shall credit the member with the largest number of full or partial months of service credit for which full payment has been made, determined in the manner provided in subsection F of § 51.1-142.2 and without regard to the method by which payment was made, valued at the standard cost determined as of the date the Board accepted the election, and shall release the reservation. A member credited under this subdivision shall not be treated as having reached his unreduced retirement eligibility date by reason of the election. Neither this sentence nor the provision of subsection E that no member shall be treated as having reached his unreduced retirement eligibility date by reason of an election before payment in full is received applies where a determination under this subdivision is rescinded under subdivision 4.</u>

<u>4. Subdivisions 2 and 3 shall not apply, and the service credit purchased under the election shall vest at the cost determined under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2, if the member, after the Board accepts the election, (i) dies, (ii) is approved for a disability retirement allowance under this chapter, or (iii) is unable to retire in a qualifying retirement by reason of an error of the Board. For the purposes of clause (iii) of this subdivision, a member who retires at a time when the Board has not given a statement or notice of the member's final qualifying retirement date required by the definition of "final qualifying retirement date" in subsection A, and the time allowed by that definition for giving that statement or notice has expired, is unable to retire in a qualifying retirement by reason of an error of the Board; and a member whose final qualifying retirement date passes at a time when the Board has not given such a statement or notice and the time allowed by that definition for giving it has expired is unable to retire in a qualifying retirement by reason of an error of the Board. For the purposes of the preceding sentence, a statement or notice of the member's final qualifying retirement date required by that definition does not include the notice of the Board's determination upon a request, and of the reasons for it, that that definition requires to be given no later than 30 days after the request is filed, and the remedies of the member for a failure of the Board to give that notice are those provided by subdivision D 1, by that definition, and by subsection M. Clause (i) of this subdivision does not apply where the member has retired in a retirement that is not a qualifying retirement before the date of death, except that a retirement that is not a qualifying retirement and the effective date of which the Board determines after the date of the member's death, otherwise than by way of correction or redetermination of a date previously determined, is not a retirement before the date of death for the purposes of this subdivision, and for those purposes the member shall not be treated as having retired before the date of death in such a retirement. In the case of death, the Board shall credit the service credit purchased and shall pay any benefit payable to the member's survivor or beneficiary. Notwithstanding the crediting required by the preceding sentence, where the member dies before completing payment, the Board shall credit the member with the largest number of full or partial months of service credit for which full payment has been made, determined in the manner provided in subsection F of § 51.1-142.2 and without regard to the method by which payment was made, valued at the cost determined under subdivision B 4 of § 51.1-142.2; shall recertify the certified subsidy to reflect the service credit so credited, and subdivision D 6 applies to that recertification only so far as it provides for the reservation of an increase; and shall refund, to the member's estate and to any state agency from whose appropriation an amount was paid on the member's behalf in proportion to the amounts each paid, any amount paid that exceeds the cost of the service credit so credited. The Board shall pay a refund required by the preceding sentence within 60 days after it credits the service credit, and shall pay the whole of that refund to the member's estate and to any state agency from whose appropriation an amount was paid on the member's behalf in proportion to the amounts each paid and shall apply no amount of it to the purchase of service credit. The Board shall credit the service credit required by this subdivision no later than 60 days after it learns of the member's death, and shall state in the report required by subsection J every failure to credit it within that period. A state agency that employs or last employed a member whose election the Board has accepted shall give the Board written notice of the member's death no later than 30 days after the agency learns of it, and the Board shall state every failure of a state agency to give that notice of which the Board becomes aware in the report required by subsection J. Notwithstanding so much of subdivision C 1 of § 51.1-165 and of subsection F of § 51.1-165.01 as requires benefits to be paid in accordance with § 51.1-163, where clause (i) of this subdivision applies and the member has not retired before the date of death, the Board shall determine the benefit payable under this subdivision under § 51.1-162 as on the death of a member in service, computed on the member's creditable service, including the service credit credited under this subdivision; and for the purposes of § 51.1-162 and of the definition of "accumulated contributions" in § 51.1-124.3 the requirement that the member be in service shall not apply and the member shall be treated as having died in service prior to retirement. Nothing in the preceding sentence reduces a benefit payable under subdivision C 2 of § 51.1-165, and where that subdivision applies and the person or persons to whom that subdivision requires a benefit to be paid are the same as the person or persons to whom the benefit determined under the preceding sentence would be payable, the Board shall pay the benefit that subdivision provides or the benefit determined under the preceding sentence, whichever is greater, the two being compared on the basis of actuarial equivalence as of the date of the member's death determined on the actuarial assumptions and factors the Board uses to determine actuarial equivalence under § 51.1-165, and for the purposes of that comparison the benefit payable in accordance with § 51.1-163 shall be determined as though the effective date of the member's retirement were the date of death, and shall pay it to that person or those persons; and where that subdivision applies and the person or persons to whom that subdivision requires a benefit to be paid are not the same as the person or persons to whom the benefit determined under the preceding sentence would be payable, the Board shall pay the benefit that subdivision provides and shall not pay the benefit determined under the preceding sentence. Where the member dies, the Board shall give written notice of the service credit credited under this subdivision, of the benefit payable to the member's survivor or beneficiary and of the computation of it, of any right under subsection C of § 51.1-162 of a beneficiary entitled to the entire amount of a retirement allowance under that subsection to waive the allowance and of the period stated in that subsection within which the waiver must be made and of any extension of that period under this subdivision, of any right under subsection B of § 51.1-162 of the person to whom an allowance is payable under that subsection to elect to be paid the member's accumulated contributions or a lump sum in lieu of any other benefit under that section, of the amount of any refund required by this subdivision, and of the reasons for the Board's determinations, to the member's estate, to each person entitled to any benefit payable on the member's death or, where that person is a minor or is a person under a disability as defined in § 8.01-2, to that person's guardian, conservator, committee, or other fiduciary within the meaning of that section and, where there is none, to the person to whom the Board pays or would pay the benefit, to any person who claimed any benefit payable on the member's death and whom the Board determined is not entitled to it, and to any state agency from whose appropriation an amount was paid on the member's behalf, no later than 30 days after the earlier of the date on which it credits the service credit and the last day on which this subdivision requires it to be credited or, as to a person whose entitlement or non-entitlement to any benefit payable on the member's death, or the amount of any benefit payable to that person, the Board determines after the earlier of those dates, no later than 30 days after that determination; a person to whom that notice is required to be given may seek review of any determination stated in it under subsection M; and the period within which such a person may seek review under subsection M of a determination stated in that notice runs from the date the notice is given to that person or, where this subdivision requires the notice to be given to another person in respect of that person, from the date it is given to that other person; and where the Board after giving that notice determines a person's entitlement or non-entitlement to any benefit payable on the member's death differently, or determines the amount of a benefit differently, it shall give that person a further notice stating the determination and the reasons for it no later than 30 days after making it, and the period within which that person may seek review under subsection M of that determination runs from the date that further notice is given. The Board shall state in the report required by subsection J every failure to give the notice required by the preceding sentence to a person within the period this subdivision allows for the giving of it to that person. Where the Board gives a notice required by this subdivision on the death of a member later than 30 days after the death, the period stated in subsection C of § 51.1-162 within which a waiver must be made does not expire before the thirtieth day after the notice is given to the person entitled to make it. Where no retirement allowance is payable under § 51.1-162 on the member's death, whether because the person entitled to any benefit payable on the member's death elects under § 51.1-162 to be paid the member's accumulated contributions or a lump sum in lieu of any other benefit under that section or because no such allowance is payable to any person or because this subdivision requires the Board not to pay the benefit determined under this subdivision, the Board shall release the amount settled under this subdivision and shall restore it to the amount available under the limitation stated in the first sentence of subdivision D 2, to the extent the amount settled has not been paid to the retirement system under subsection N and to the extent it has not already been released and not reinstated, no later than 60 days after the election or, where no such allowance is payable to any person or where this subdivision requires the Board not to pay the benefit determined under this subdivision, no later than 60 days after the Board determines that no such allowance is payable or that this subdivision requires it not to pay that benefit, as the case may be, notwithstanding the requirement of subdivision D 2 that an amount settled remain charged against that limitation. Where an amount released under the preceding sentence would not have been released had a determination of the Board been as it stands after reconsideration under subsection M or after judicial review, the Board shall reinstate the amount released no later than 30 days after the determination is reconsidered or reviewed, the amount so reinstated shall be charged against the limitations in subdivision D 2 notwithstanding that the amount available to be reserved is thereby exceeded, and the rule of this subdivision that no certified subsidy is settled more than once does not prevent the settlement of an amount so reinstated. Subsection E does not prevent the crediting of service credit required by this subdivision. Where the member is approved for a disability retirement allowance, the Board shall recertify the certified subsidy to reflect the commencement of a disability retirement allowance, and subdivision D 6 applies to that recertification only so far as it provides for the reservation of an increase. The certified subsidy for an election to which clause (i), (ii), or (iii) of this subdivision applies and that is not settled under subdivision 1 is settled, and subsection N applies to that settlement as it applies to a settlement under subdivision 1, on the earliest of (a) the date of the member's death, (b) the effective date of a disability retirement allowance approved for the member, and (c) the effective date of a retirement that the member is unable to take as a qualifying retirement by reason of an error of the Board. The Board shall make every recertification required by this subdivision before making the redetermination required by this subdivision, except that where a recertification becomes required after the redetermination has been made the Board shall make the recertification and shall thereupon redetermine the certified subsidy again as this subdivision requires. The Board shall, no later than 90 days after the date on which the certified subsidy is settled under subdivision 1 or under this subdivision, as the case may be, or, where that date is the date of the member's death, no later than 90 days after the date on which the Board learns of the death, redetermine the certified subsidy in the manner provided in subdivision 1 but substituting that date for the member's actual effective date of retirement, the amount settled shall be the amount so redetermined or the amount last certified and reserved for the election, whichever is less, and the Board shall release the amount by which the amount reserved for the election, together with the contingency amount reserved under subdivision D 3, exceeds the amount settled.
