> **STORE REVISION 66 — [MOO-182](/MOO/issues/MOO-182)'s COUNSEL FINDINGS A4 AND A2 CUT IN, BOTH ACCEPTED IN FULL AND NEITHER REFUSED.** One inserted
> sentence in subdivision G 4, ten substituted anchors across subdivisions D 6, G 2, G 4 and M 2 of
> § 51.1-142.4, an actor for subdivision B 4 e of § 51.1-142.2, one new defined term, and four
> conforming amendments the sweep required and MOO-196 did not list. **+825 characters, reconciled edit
> by edit with no residue. No figure, threshold, percentage or date moves; no designator is renumbered;
> no section joins or leaves the act; not one word of existing non-underlined text is struck or
> reworded; no existing sentence boundary moves; and the title line does not change.** Narrative
> **§0.0-66**; sweep **§5.44**; drafter's note **§6.0-66**.
>
> **A4 — subdivision G 4 no longer commands the Board to undo its own commanded releases.** The
> reinstatement sentence was keyed to the *complement* of subdivision D 7, a closed list that **none** of
> the act's commanded releases is on — subdivision G 1's release of the excess over the amount settled,
> subdivision G 4's own redetermination release, subdivision D 3's contingency release on settlement,
> and **a fourth the finding does not list, G 4's death-case release.** So on every ordinary, correct
> settlement the Program produced, the Board had to reinstate within 60 days of learning of its own
> release, charge it back over the self-executing **$3,000,000** bar, tell both money-committee Chairmen
> *"the nature of any error"* where there was none, notify every waiting-list applicant, certify an
> excess to the Governor and **accept no further election until the excess is eliminated** — a
> self-executing shutdown of the pilot triggered by the pilot working. **One inserted sentence,
> counsel's words verbatim, scoped by `For the purposes of the next sentence`, deems a release the act
> required or permitted to be a release in accordance with subdivision D 7.** Deeming in rather than
> excluding out, so a commanded release that followed a determination later rescinded or reversed
> **still comes back**. **The four independent operative uses of `in accordance with subdivision D 7`
> and the involuntary-separation borrowing all read after the cut exactly as before** — the criterion
> this edit had to meet, verified by count.
>
> **A2 — *"employer"* no longer resolves to the Commonwealth, and subdivision B 4 e has an actor.**
> *"Employer"* was undefined in § 51.1-142.4, so § 51.1-124.3 governed and it meant **the Commonwealth**
> in every case the Program can produce — which made *"in proportion to the amounts each paid"*
> meaningless and put the Commonwealth in subdivision M 2's reconsideration and judicial-review classes
> against its own Board, with the Attorney General entitled to appear on both sides. **All ten
> `employer that paid` anchors are now `state agency from whose appropriation an amount was paid`,
> including the single curly-apostrophe occurrence** — caught by the same substitution as the other
> nine, because the anchor was chosen to stop before the apostrophe rather than work around it.
> **Subdivision B 4 e's policy duty, which named no one with power to adopt, file or publish it for the
> Commonwealth, is now the duty of the state agency that employs the member**, with *"state agency"*
> newly defined in subdivision B 4 a by reference to subsection A of § 51.1-142.4. **The five-percent

### 8.1 Status at store revision 51 and earlier — retained; moved to the retained-record document in store revision 58

**The status notes for store revisions 50, 49, 48, 47, 46 and 45 are moved verbatim** to [`bill-text-s8-revision-history-2`](/MOO/issues/MOO-42#document-bill-text-s8-revision-history-2), every blockquote, figure and caveat preserved, so a reference to the status note for any of those revisions still resolves there. **§8 retains the current note and the four before it**, which is the retention the Director's answer to §7 item 53 set and which §8 had let slip to ten. **Store revision 62 made it six**, store revision 63 made it seven without saying so, store revision 64 added none, store revision 65 made it eight, store revision 67 said nine and **omitted §8's own note for store revision 66**, store revision 68 added a note without amending this sentence, and **store revision 69 makes it twelve, counted mechanically** — notes for store revisions 69, 68, 67, 66, 65, 63, 62, 61, 60, 59, 58 and 57 — because the ceiling no longer forces a move and moving another run's record in the heartbeat it was written is how a record gets double-stubbed. **The notes for store revisions 57 and 58 are the two to move first.**

**Why, and what it buys.** §7 item 53 is not a recommendation but the standing precondition for every further revision: this file has had to move a record to fit in store revisions 34, 36, 38, 40, 41, 47, 50 and 55. This revision moves §5.32, §5.33, §6.0-53, §6.0-54 and this section, and the headroom on departure is stated in §0.0-58's companion record. **No operative text moved. §1 is untouched by any of it.**
